\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\end{subarray}}\max_{\beta>0}\max_{\|\bm{\nu}\|=\beta}\bigg(\big\langle\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\bm{g}/\sqrt{n}-\bm{\varepsilon},\bm{\nu}/\beta\big\rangle-\langle\bm{H},\bm{W}\rangle/\sqrt{n}\bigg)\beta-\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}+\lambda\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}

(306)

\displaystyle=\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathcal{W}\\
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\end{subarray}}\max_{\beta>0}\bigg(\big\|\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\bm{g}-\sqrt{n}\bm{\varepsilon}\big\|-\langle\bm{H},\bm{W}\rangle\bigg)\beta-\frac{n}{2}\beta^{2}+\lambda\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}

(307)

\displaystyle=\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathcal{W}\\
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\end{subarray}}\max_{\beta>0}\bigg(\sqrt{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}+n\sigma^{2}}\|\bm{g}\|-\langle\bm{H},\bm{W}\rangle\bigg)\beta-\frac{n}{2}\beta^{2}+\lambda\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\},

(308)

where we slightly abuse notation and let
\bm{g}
be a new Gaussian random variable with identity covariance. Dividing through by
dr
we obtain

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\mathcal{W})

\displaystyle=\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathcal{W}\\
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\end{subarray}}\max_{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{dr}\sqrt{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}+n\sigma^{2}}\|\bm{g}\|-\frac{\langle{\bm{H},\bm{W}}\rangle}{dr}\bigg)\beta-\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{2}}{2}+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}

(309)

\displaystyle=\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathcal{W}\\
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\end{subarray}}\max_{\beta}\bigg(\gamma_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{\langle{\bm{H},\bm{W}}\rangle}{dr}\bigg)\beta-\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{2}}{2}+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}

(310)

\displaystyle=\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathcal{W}\\
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{\gamma_{n}}{2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{W}\rangle}{n}\bigg)_{+}^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}.

(311)

The following lemma uses the Matrix CGMT (
Theorem

3
) to demonstrate that the minimizer of
C_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{W})
over any set
\mathcal{D}
is close to
L_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\mathcal{D})
with comparable probability.

Lemma 13
.

Let
\mathcal{D}
be any closed subset of
\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
. Then for all
t\in\mathbb{R}
it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathcal{D}:\bm{W+M}\succcurlyeq 0}\frac{1}{dr}\mathcal{C}^{(n)}_{\lambda}(\bm{W})\leq t\bigg\}\leq 2\mathbb{P}\bigg\{L^{(n)}_{\lambda}(\mathcal{D})\leq t\bigg\}.

(312)

If
\mathcal{D}
is also convex, then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathcal{D}:\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0}\frac{1}{dr}\mathcal{C}^{(n)}_{\lambda}(\bm{W})>t\bigg\}\leq 2\mathbb{P}\bigg\{L^{(n)}_{\lambda}(\mathcal{D})>t\bigg\};

(313)

Proof.

See
Section

C.1
.
∎

We will also need the following lemma which relates, for appropriate choice of
\mathcal{D}
, the quantity
L^{(n)}_{\lambda}(\mathcal{D})
with
L_{\lambda}^{*}
, where we define

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{*}

\displaystyle=\max_{\beta}\min_{\tau}\psi_{\lambda}^{*}(\tau,\beta)=\psi_{\lambda}^{*}(\tau^{*},\beta^{*});

(314)

\displaystyle\psi^{*}_{\lambda}(\tau,\beta)

\displaystyle:=\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau}+\tau\bigg)\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta}{2}-\gamma_{n}\frac{\beta^{2}}{2}+\mathbb{E}\min_{\bm{Z}}\frac{\|\bm{Z}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}{2dr\tau}\beta+\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{Z}-\bm{M}\rangle}{dr}\beta+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big(\|\bm{Z}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big).

(315)

Here
\beta^{*}=\zeta^{*}\tau^{*}
, where
(\tau^{*},\zeta^{*})
solve the fixed-point equations.

Lemma 14
.

Suppose
1
,
2
and
3
are satisfied. Then for any
\varepsilon
satisfying
\varepsilon^{2}\geq C\exp(-cdr)
with probability at least

1-O\bigg(\exp(-cd)+\exp(-cdr)+\exp(-cn)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\exp(-cdr\varepsilon^{4})-\exp(-\frac{(dr)^{2}}{n}\varepsilon^{4})\bigg)
it holds that

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\bigg)\leq L_{\lambda}^{*}+C\varepsilon^{2};

(316)

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\mathbb{B}_{\varepsilon/2}(\bm{\widehat{W}}/\sqrt{dr})^{c}\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\cap\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\bigg)>L_{\lambda}^{*}+2C\varepsilon^{2}

(317)

Proof.

See
Section

C.2
.
∎

Proof of
Theorem

4
.

Observe that because

\displaystyle C^{(n)}(\bm{W})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\|\mathcal{X}(\bm{W})-\bm{\varepsilon}\|_{F}^{2}+\lambda\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\lambda\|\bm{M}\|_{*},

(318)

we need to argue that any minimizer of
C^{(n)}(\bm{W})
(over the shifted cone
\bm{M}+\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}_{\succcurlyeq 0})
must must be within
\varepsilon
of
\bm{\widehat{W}}
with high probability. Let
\mathcal{D}
be the set

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}:=\big\{\bm{W}:\bigg|\phi\big(\frac{\bm{M}+\bm{W}}{\sqrt{dr}}\big)-\mathbb{E}\big[\phi\big(\frac{\bm{M}+\bm{W}}{\sqrt{dr}}\big)\big]\bigg|\leq\varepsilon/2\big\}\cap\{\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\}.

(319)

Then we need to show that the minimizer of
C^{(n)}
lies within
\mathcal{D}
with high probability. Let
\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}
denote the
\varepsilon
-enlargement of the set
\mathcal{D}
; i.e.,
\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}:=\{\bm{W}:\inf_{\bm{W}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{D}}\|\bm{W}-\bm{W}^{\prime}\|_{F}<\varepsilon\}
. We will show that any rank at most
r
minimum of
C^{(n)}
on
\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}^{c}
(i.e. outside an
\varepsilon/2
-region of
\mathcal{D}
) must be suboptimal with high probability, which implies that the minimizer lies within
\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}
. To accomplish this, first note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\argmin C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\notin\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\argmin C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\notin\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}\cap\mathcal{E}_{{\sf Good}}\bigg\}+\mathbb{P}\mathcal{E}_{{\sf Good}}^{c}.

(320)

Observe that the first event above satisfies

\displaystyle\bigg\{

\displaystyle\argmin C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\notin\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}\cap\mathcal{E}_{{\sf Good}}\bigg\}

(321)

\displaystyle\subset\bigg\{\argmin C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\notin\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\cap\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\bigg\}

(322)

\displaystyle\subset\bigg\{\exists\bm{W}:\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0,\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r,\bm{W}\notin\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2},\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr},

(323)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\leq\min_{\bm{W}:\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\bigg\}

(324)

\displaystyle\subset\bigg\{\exists\bm{W}:\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0,\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r,\bm{W}\in\mathcal{D}^{c}_{\varepsilon/2},\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr},

(325)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\leq\min_{\bm{W}:\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(326)

\displaystyle=\bigg\{\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathcal{D}^{c}_{\varepsilon/2}\\
\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\\
\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\end{subarray}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\leq\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}\\
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\\
\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\end{subarray}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}.

(327)

As a result,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\argmin C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\notin\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}\bigg\}

(328)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathcal{D}^{c}_{\varepsilon/2}\\
\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\\
\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\end{subarray}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\leq\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}\\
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\\
\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\end{subarray}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(329)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\big\{\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathcal{D}^{c}_{\varepsilon/2}\\
\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\\
\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\end{subarray}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\leq\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}\\
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\\
\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\end{subarray}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})+C\varepsilon^{2}\}

(330)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\bigcap\{\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}\\
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\\
\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\end{subarray}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\leq L^{*}_{\lambda}+C\varepsilon^{2}\big\}\bigg\}

(331)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}:\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0,\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\geq L^{*}_{\lambda}+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(332)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathcal{D}^{c}_{\varepsilon/2}\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\leq L^{*}_{\lambda}+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(333)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}:\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\geq L^{*}_{\lambda}+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(334)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{P}\bigg\{L_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}^{c}\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\cap\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr})\leq L^{*}_{\lambda}+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(335)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{P}\bigg\{L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\mathbb{B}(0,C\sigma\sqrt{dr})\big)\geq L^{*}_{\lambda}+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\},

(336)

where the final inequality is by
Lemma

13
. Next, note that either
\bm{\widehat{W}}\notin\mathcal{D}
or
\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}^{c}\subset\mathbb{B}_{\varepsilon/2}\big(\bm{\widehat{W}}\big)^{c}\cap\{\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\}.
As a result,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{L_{\lambda}^{(n)}

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}^{c}\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r)\cap\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\big)\leq L_{\lambda}^{*}+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(337)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\big\{\bm{\widehat{W}}\notin\mathcal{D}\big\}+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{L_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\mathbb{B}_{\varepsilon/2}\big(\bm{\widehat{W}}\big)^{c}\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r)\cap\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\big)\leq L^{*}_{\lambda}+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}.

(338)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon/2}^{c}}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})\leq\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}:\bm{W}-\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0}C^{(n)}(\bm{W})+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(339)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{P}\big\{\bm{\widehat{W}}\notin\mathcal{D}\big\}+2\mathbb{P}\bigg\{L_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\mathbb{B}_{\varepsilon/2}\big(\bm{\widehat{W}}\big)^{c}\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r)\big)\leq L^{*}_{\lambda}+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(340)

\displaystyle\quad+2\mathbb{P}\bigg\{L_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\mathbb{R}^{d\times d})\geq L_{\lambda}^{*}+C\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

(341)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{P}\big\{\bm{\widehat{W}}\notin\mathcal{D}\big\}+O\bigg(\exp(-cd)+\exp(-cdr)+\exp(-cn)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\exp(-cdr\varepsilon^{4})-\exp(-\frac{(dr)^{2}}{n}\varepsilon^{4})\bigg),

(342)

where we have applied
Lemma

14
.
Note that by construction
\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0
almost surely. In addition, we have that the soft-thresholding operator is a proximal operator and hence nonexpansive, implying it is 1-Lipschitz. As a result, the function

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{dr}}\bm{M}-\frac{\tau^{*}}{\sqrt{dr}}\bm{H}\mapsto\bm{\widehat{W}}

(343)

is a
\tau^{*}/\sqrt{dr}
Lipschitz function of
\bm{H}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big\{\bm{\widehat{W}}\notin\mathcal{D}\big\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\big\{-c\frac{dr\varepsilon^{2}}{\tau^{*}}\big\}.

(344)

By
Lemma

6
,
\sigma\leq\tau^{*}\leq C\sigma
, and hence by adjusting constants if necessary, we complete the proof.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

13

Proof of
Lemma

13
.

The proof amounts to rewriting the appropriate quantities.
We have that

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}:\bm{W+M}\succcurlyeq 0\cap\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{W})

\displaystyle=\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}:\bm{W+M}\succcurlyeq 0\cap\mathcal{D}}\frac{1}{2}\|\mathcal{X}[\bm{W}]-\bm{\varepsilon}\|_{F}^{2}+\lambda\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}

(345)

\displaystyle=\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}:\bm{W+M}\succcurlyeq 0\cap\mathcal{D}}\max_{\bm{\nu}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}}\langle\mathcal{X}[\bm{W}],\bm{\nu}\rangle-\langle\bm{\varepsilon},\bm{\nu}\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\nu}\|^{2}+\lambda\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}.

(346)

Furthermore, observe that
\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{W})
is convex and satisfies
\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{W})\to\infty
as
\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\to\infty
, and hence admits minimizers. Therefore, there exists a constant
M
sufficiently large such that the maximizer
\nu
is bounded with
\|\bm{\nu}\|\leq M
. As a result, since the min-max is attained on a closed set, we may apply the MCGMT to deduce that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}:\bm{W+M}\succcurlyeq 0\cap\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{W})>t\bigg\}

(347)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\min_{\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}:\bm{W+M}\succcurlyeq 0\cap\mathcal{D}}\max_{\bm{\nu}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}}\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\langle\bm{g},\bm{\nu}\rangle/\sqrt{n}+\|\bm{\nu}\|\langle\bm{H},\bm{W}\rangle/\sqrt{n}-\langle\bm{\varepsilon},\bm{\nu}\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\nu}\|^{2}+\lambda\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}>t\bigg\},

(348)

where the final equality is due to the fact that
-\langle\bm{\varepsilon},\bm{\nu}\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\nu}\|^{2}
is concave (and hence the function
(\bm{W},\bm{\nu})\mapsto-\langle\bm{\varepsilon},\bm{\nu}\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\nu}\|^{2}+\lambda\|\bm{W}\|_{*}
is convex-concave). Here
\bm{g}
is a standard Gaussian random vector and
\bm{H}
is a GOE matrix. The first equality follows from the fact that
\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{v}\|_{2}^{2}=\max_{\bm{\nu}}\langle\bm{v},\bm{\nu}\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\nu}\|^{2}
. Since the statement is probabilistic in nature, we are free to make the replacement
\bm{H}\mapsto-\bm{H}
by symmetry. Diving the resulting expressions inside the probability by
dr
and recognizing that the right hand side is simply
\psi^{(n)}_{\lambda}(\bm{W},\bm{\nu})
, we complete the proof (after rescaling
t
by
dr
). The reverse inequality follows by the same argument.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

14

To prove this result we first require the following lemma.

Lemma 15
.

Suppose
1
,
2
and
3
are satisfied. Let
R=C\sigma\sqrt{dr}
, with
C
as in
Lemma

3
.
Then with probability at least

1-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\exp(-cn\varepsilon^{2})-\exp(-dr\varepsilon^{2})

it holds that

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R;\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\bigg)\geq L_{\lambda}^{*}-C\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}\varepsilon.

(349)

Proof.

See
Section

C.2.1
.
∎

In order to give the proof we will require some additional notation. Define the function
h:\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}\to\mathbb{R}
as follows. First, set

\displaystyle f

\displaystyle:\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}\to\mathbb{R}

(350)

\displaystyle\bm{U}

\displaystyle\mapsto\sqrt{\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n}.

(351)

Define

\displaystyle h(\bm{U}):

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma_{n}}{2}(f(\bm{U}))^{2}_{+}+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{U}\|_{F}^{2}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}.

(352)

Recall we denote
\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U},\bm{U}^{\prime}}
via

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U},\bm{U}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\argmin_{\mathcal{OO}^{\top}=\bm{I}_{r}}\|\bm{U}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U}^{\prime}\|_{F}.

(353)

The following lemma shows that
h(\bm{U})
is strongly convex on a high probability event.

Lemma 16
.

Suppose
1
,
2
and
3
are satisfied.
Let
h(\bm{U})
be defined in
(
352
)
. Let
\bm{M}=\bm{U}^{*}\bm{U}^{*\top}
with
\bm{U}^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
. Define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{h}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{\big|\langle\bm{H},\bm{Q}\rangle\big|\leq C\sqrt{dr}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}\text{ for all matrices $\bm{Q}$ of rank at most $2r$}\bigg\}\cap\big\{\frac{1}{2}\leq\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\leq 2\big\}\cap\bigg\{\|\bm{H}\|\leq 3\sqrt{d}\bigg\}.

(354)

Then for all
\bm{U}
and
\bm{V}=\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\prime}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{\prime}\bm{U}}
satisfying

\displaystyle\max\bigg\{\|\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{*}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}}\|,\|\bm{U}^{\prime}-\bm{U}^{*}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}^{\prime}}\|\bigg\}\leq\frac{c}{\kappa(\bm{M})}\|\bm{U}^{*}\|,

(355)

for some sufficiently small constant
c
,
it holds that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\geq\bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\min}\big(\bm{M}\big)}{32dr}+2\frac{\lambda}{dr}\bigg)\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(356)

Proof.

See
Section

C.2.2
.
∎

In addition, the following additional lemma shows that
\mathcal{E}_{h}
above holds with high probability.

Lemma 17
.

The event
\mathcal{E}_{h}
holds with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cdr)-2\exp(-cn)
.

Proof.

See
Section

C.2.3
.
∎

Finally, the following lemma shows that when
\bm{U}\in\mathcal{R}
satisfies
h(\bm{U})\leq\min_{\bm{U}\in\mathcal{R}}h(\bm{U})+\varepsilon
, it holds that
\bm{\widehat{U}}
must be close to
\bm{U}
.

Lemma 18
.

Let
h:\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}\to\mathbb{R}
be a function such that
h(\bm{U}\mathcal{O})=h(\bm{U})
for all orthogonal matrices
\mathcal{O}
.
Let
\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},K)
denote the set of matrices
\bm{U}
such that
\|\bm{U}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U},\bm{\widehat{U}}}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\leq K
.
Suppose that for all
\bm{U}\in\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},r)
it holds that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\geq\gamma\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2},

(357)

where
\bm{V}=\bm{U}_{1}-\bm{U}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U},\bm{U}_{1}}
. Suppose further that

\displaystyle h(\bm{\widehat{U}})\leq\min_{\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},K)}h(\bm{U})+\varepsilon.

(358)

Then if
\bm{U}_{0}
is any minimizer of
h
over this set, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\widehat{U}}-\bm{U}_{0}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}_{0},\bm{\widehat{U}}}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\varepsilon.

(359)

Furthermore, it must be that for all
\bm{\widetilde{U}}\in\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},K)
,
h(\bm{\widetilde{U}})\leq\min_{\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},K)}h(\bm{U})+\varepsilon
and
\|\bm{\widetilde{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widetilde{U}},\bm{\widehat{U}}}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{8}{\gamma}\varepsilon
.

Proof.

See
Section

C.2.4
.
∎

We also need the following result.

Lemma 19
.

Suppose
1
,
2
and
3
are satisfied.
With probability at least
1-O\bigg(\exp\big(-Cdr\big(C\exp(-cdr)+\varepsilon\big)^{2}\big)+\exp(-cdr)\bigg)
it holds that
L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\big)\leq L_{\lambda}^{*}+\varepsilon+C\exp(-cdr)
.

Proof.

See
Section

C.2.5
.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

14
.

Recall that we aim to show that:

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\cap PSD\bigg)\leq L_{\lambda}^{*}+C\varepsilon^{2};

(360)

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\mathbb{B}_{\varepsilon/2}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{W}}/\sqrt{dr}\bigg)^{c}\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W+M})\leq r\cap\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\bigg)>L_{\lambda}^{*}+C\varepsilon^{2}

(361)

with high probability.
We split up the proof into steps.

Step 1: Good events.
First, let
\mathcal{E}_{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:Zstgoodproperties}}}
denote the event from
Lemma

11
. On this event, it holds that
\mathrm{rank}(\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*}))\leq r
, whence the quantity
\bm{\widehat{U}}
is well-defined on this event. When
\bm{\widehat{U}}
is well-defined, define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}:=\bigg\{h(\bm{\widehat{U}})\leq L_{\lambda}^{*}+C\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}\widetilde{}\varepsilon\bigg\},

(362)

where
\widetilde{}\varepsilon
will be chosen momentarily. We also define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}:=\bigg\{L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\cap\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\cap\mathrm{Rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\big)\geq L_{\lambda}^{*}-C\widetilde{}\varepsilon\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}\bigg\},

(363)

where
C
is defined as in
Lemma

15
. In fact, by this same lemma, we have on the event therein and
\mathcal{E}_{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:Zstgoodproperties}}}
it holds that

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\cap\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\cap\mathrm{Rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\big)

\displaystyle\geq L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\cap\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\big)

(364)

\displaystyle\geq L_{\lambda}^{*}-C\widetilde{}\varepsilon\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}.

(365)

Step 2: The bound (
316
).
We have that
on these events

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{*}+C\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}\widetilde{}\varepsilon\geq h(\bm{\widehat{U}})

\displaystyle\geq L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\cap PSD\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\big)

(366)

\displaystyle\geq L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\cap PSD\big),

(367)

which proves (
316
) whenever
C\widetilde{}\varepsilon\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}=C^{\prime}\varepsilon^{2}
, which we will show is valid at the end of Step 3 below.

Step 3: The bound (
317
).
The bound (
317
) will require a little more analysis.
Define the regions

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}:=\{\bm{U}:\|\bm{U}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{8\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}R\};

(368)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}:=\{\bm{U}:\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}\leq R\}.

(369)

We claim that have the containment
\mathcal{R}_{1}\subset\mathcal{R}_{2}
on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:Zstgoodproperties}}}
.
In order to see this, observe that for any
\bm{U}\in\mathcal{R}_{1}
,

\displaystyle\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\leq\big(\|\bm{U}\|+\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\|\big)\frac{1}{8\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}R.

(370)

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:Zstgoodproperties}}}
it holds that
\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda)}\|\leq 2\|\bm{M}\|
. Consequently, on this same event, we have that
\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\|\leq 2\|\bm{U}^{*}\|
, since
\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\|^{2}=\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})\|
.
Furthermore, we have that by virtue of
\bm{U}\in\mathcal{R}_{1}
,

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U},\bm{\widehat{U}}}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\|+\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\|\leq\frac{1}{8\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}R+2\|\bm{U}^{*}\|

(371)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{8\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}(\bm{M})}}+2\|\bm{U}^{*}\|\leq 4\|\bm{U}^{*}\|.

(372)

Consequently,

\displaystyle\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}\leq\big(\|\bm{U}\|+\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\|\big)\frac{1}{8\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}R\leq R.

(373)

Therefore,
\mathcal{R}_{1}\subset\mathcal{R}_{2}
. Note that we have used the implicit assumption that
\lambda_{r}\geq C_{1}\sigma\sqrt{r}
for some sufficiently large constant
C_{1}
.

We will now show that
h
is strongly convex on the region
\mathcal{R}_{2}
for a particular parameter
\alpha
. By
Lemma

16
on the event
\mathcal{E}_{h}
,
h
is strongly convex with the parameter

\displaystyle\alpha=\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{32dr}+2\frac{\lambda}{dr},

(374)

provided
\bm{U}
lies in the region
\mathcal{R}
defined via

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}:=\bigg\{\bm{U}:\|\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{*}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}}\|\leq\frac{c}{\kappa(\bm{M})}\|\bm{U}^{*}\|.\bigg\}

(375)

We will show that
\mathcal{R}_{2}\subset\mathcal{R}
.
It holds that
\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}
on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:Zstgoodproperties}}}
by appropriately choosing
s=O(1)
in the statement therein. (See, for example, the proof of
Lemma

19
.) We then have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{*}\bm{U}^{*\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq R+\|\bm{U}^{*}\bm{U}^{*\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}=R+\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda)}-\bm{M}\|_{F}

(376)

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\sigma\sqrt{dr}.

(377)

As a consequence, we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{*}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}}\|\leq\frac{c_{0}C^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\min}}}\sigma\sqrt{dr}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\kappa(\bm{M})}\|\bm{U}^{*}\|,

(378)

which holds as long as
\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})/\sigma\geq C_{1}\kappa\sqrt{dr}
, for some appropriately large constant
C_{1}
, which holds according to
1
.

Consequently, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:Zstgoodproperties}}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{h}
,

\displaystyle h(\bm{\widehat{U}})

\displaystyle=L_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{\widehat{W}})\leq L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\cap\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0\cap\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\big)+2C\widetilde{}\varepsilon\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}

(379)

\displaystyle=\min_{\mathcal{R}_{2}}h(\bm{U})+2C\widetilde{}\varepsilon\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}

(380)

\displaystyle\leq\min_{\mathcal{R}_{1}}h(\bm{U})+2C\widetilde{}\varepsilon\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}

(381)

\displaystyle=\min_{\bm{U}:\|\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{*}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}}\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{8\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}R}h(\bm{U})+2C\widetilde{}\varepsilon\sqrt{\gamma_{n}},

(382)

were the final bound holds from the containment
\mathcal{R}_{1}\subset\mathcal{R}_{2}
. By
Lemma

18
, since
h
is strongly convex on the region
\mathcal{R}_{2}
with parameter
\alpha
, for any
\bm{U}\in\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},\frac{R}{8\|\bm{U}^{*}\|})
, it holds that
\|\bm{U}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U},\bm{\widehat{U}}}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{8C\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}\widetilde{}\varepsilon}{\alpha}
.
In addition, for any
\bm{U}\in\mathbb{B}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}},\frac{R}{8\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}\big)
, we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}\leq\big(\|\bm{U}\|+\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\|\big)\|\bm{U}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}\|_{F}\leq 4\|\bm{U}^{*}\|\|\bm{U}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}\|_{F}.

(383)

Consequently, if

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{dr}}>\frac{\varepsilon}{2},

(384)

then it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}\|_{F}\geq\frac{1}{4\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}>\frac{\varepsilon\sqrt{dr}}{4\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}.

(385)

The contrapositive of
Lemma

18
implies that for
\bm{U}
as above, it must hold that

\displaystyle h(\bm{U})>\min_{\bm{U}:\|\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{*}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}}\|_{F}\leq\frac{R}{8\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}}h(\bm{U})+\bigg(\frac{\varepsilon\sqrt{dr}}{4\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}\bigg)^{2}\frac{\alpha}{8}

(386)

Define
\widetilde{}\varepsilon
via

\displaystyle\widetilde{}\varepsilon=\frac{\varepsilon^{2}dr\alpha}{128C\sqrt{\gamma}_{n}\|\bm{U}^{*}\|^{2}}.

(387)

Then the display equation (
386
) simplifies to

\displaystyle h(\bm{U})>\min_{\bm{U}:\|\bm{U-U^{*}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}}\|_{F}\leq\frac{R}{8\|\bm{U}^{*}\|}}h(\bm{U})+C\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}\widetilde{}\varepsilon,

(388)

which is a contradiction to (
382
). Recalling the definition of
\alpha
from earlier, we have that

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}\widetilde{}\varepsilon

\displaystyle=\frac{\varepsilon^{2}dr}{128\|\bm{U}^{*}\|^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{32dr}+2\frac{\lambda}{dr}\bigg)

(389)

\displaystyle=\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{128}\bigg(\frac{1}{32\kappa}+2\frac{\lambda}{\|\bm{M}\|}\bigg)=C\varepsilon^{2}.

(390)

which justifies the previous choice of
\widetilde{}\varepsilon
from the previous step.
In particular, on these events it holds that

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\mathbb{B}_{\varepsilon/2}^{c}(\bm{\widehat{W}}/\sqrt{dr})\cap\mathrm{rank}(W+M)\leq r\cap\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R\bigg)>L_{\lambda}^{*}+2\varepsilon^{2}

(391)

which is precisely (
317
). This algebra also verifies the choice of
\widetilde{}\varepsilon
from the previous step of the proof.

Step 4: Tabulating probabilities
.
We bound each event in turn.

•

The events
\mathcal{E}_{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:Zstgoodproperties}}
and
\mathcal{E}_{h}
: The first event holds with probability at least
1-C\exp(-cd)
. Furthermore, by
Lemma

17
,

\mathcal{E}_{h}
holds with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cdr)-2\exp(-cn)
.

•

The event
\mathcal{E}_{1}
: From
Lemma

19
we have that

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\big)\leq L_{\lambda}^{*}+s+C\exp(-cdr)

(392)

with probability at least

1-O\bigg(\exp\big(-Cdr\big(C\exp(-cdr)+s\big)^{2}\big)+\exp(-cdr)\bigg)

Let
\varepsilon^{2}=s+C\exp(-cdr)
. Then we have that

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\big(\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\big)\leq L_{\lambda}^{*}+\varepsilon^{2}

(393)

with probability at least
1-O(\exp(-cdr\varepsilon^{2})+\exp(-cdr))
as long as
\varepsilon^{2}\geq C\exp(-cdr)
, as in the statement of the proof.

•

The event
\mathcal{E}_{2}
: From
Lemma

15
, it holds that

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\|\bm{W}\|\leq R;\bm{W+M}\succcurlyeq 0\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq L_{\lambda}^{*}-Ct\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}

(394)

with probability at least
1-\frac{1}{t}\exp(-cnt^{2})-\exp(-drt^{2})
.

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\|\bm{W}\|\leq R;\bm{W+M}\succcurlyeq 0\bigg)\geq L_{\lambda}^{*}-C\varepsilon^{2}

(395)

with probability at least
1-O\big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\exp(-cdr\varepsilon^{4})-\exp(-\frac{(dr)^{2}}{n}\varepsilon^{4})
. Since

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R;\bm{W}+\bm{M}\succcurlyeq 0;\mathrm{rank}(\bm{W}+\bm{M})\leq r\bigg)\geq L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\|\bm{W}\|\leq R;\bm{W+M}\succcurlyeq 0\bigg)

(396)

always holds, we see that
\mathcal{E}_{2}
holds with this same probability.

Combining these bounds we have that the overall probability is at least

\displaystyle 1-O\bigg(\exp(-cd)+\exp(-cdr)+\exp(-cn)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\exp(-cdr\varepsilon^{4})-\exp(-\frac{(dr)^{2}}{n}\varepsilon^{4})\bigg),

(397)

which completes the proof.
∎

C.2.1
Proof of
Lemma

15

Proof of
Lemma

15
.

The proof of this result resembles the proof of Lemma B.3 in
Celentano et al. 2023
. Throughout we assume implicitly that
\bm{W}\succcurlyeq-\bm{M}
.
Throughout the proof, denote
R:=C\sigma\sqrt{dr}
.
We observe that we can rewrite
L_{\lambda}(\mathcal{W})
(see the derivation in (
308
)) via

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}(\bm{W})

\displaystyle:=\max_{\beta>0}\bigg(\gamma_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{W}\rangle}{dr}\bigg)\beta-\gamma_{n}\frac{\beta^{2}}{2}+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}

(398)

\displaystyle=:\max_{\beta\geq 0}\ell_{\lambda}(\bm{W},\beta;\bm{g})

(399)

\displaystyle\geq\ell_{\lambda}(\bm{W},\beta^{*};\bm{g}).

(400)

Define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{g}}}:=\bigg\{\bigg|\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-1\bigg|\leq t/\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}\bigg\}.

(401)

Since
\bm{g}\mapsto\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}
is a 1-Lipschitz function, we have that
\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{g}}}\big)\geq 1-\exp(-cdrt^{2})
for all
t>0
.
Denote the modified objective

\displaystyle\widetilde{\ell}_{\lambda}(\bm{W},\beta):=\bigg(\gamma_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{W}\rangle}{dr}\bigg)\beta-\gamma_{n}\frac{\beta^{2}}{2}+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}.

(402)

If
\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R
, it holds that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\bm{g}}
,

\displaystyle\big|\ell_{\lambda}(\bm{W},\beta^{*})-\widetilde{\ell}_{\lambda}(\bm{W},\beta)\big|\leq\beta_{\max}\gamma_{n}^{1/2}t\sqrt{\frac{R^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}}\leq 2C\gamma_{n}^{1/2}t\sigma.

(403)

Consequently, on this event

\displaystyle\min_{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R}L_{\lambda}(\bm{W})

\displaystyle=\min_{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R}\max_{\beta\geq 0}\ell(\bm{W},\beta)\geq\min_{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R}\ell(\bm{W},\beta^{*})

(404)

\displaystyle\geq\min_{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R}\widetilde{\ell}(\bm{W},\beta^{*})-2\beta_{\max}\gamma_{n}^{1/2}\varepsilon\sigma.

(405)

Next, for every
\tau\in[\sigma,\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\frac{R^{2}}{n}}]
, the function

\displaystyle\bm{H}\mapsto\min_{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R}\bigg\{\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}\|\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\tau n}-\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{n}\langle\bm{H},\bm{W}\rangle+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\}\bigg\}

(406)

is
\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{n}R
-Lipschitz, and hence so is
\bm{H}\mapsto F(\tau,\bm{H})
. Consequently,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|F(\tau,\bm{H})-\mathbb{E}F(\tau,\bm{H})\bigg|>\varepsilon\frac{R\gamma_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg(-c\frac{n}{(\beta^{*})^{2}}\varepsilon^{2}\bigg).

(407)

Furthermore, since
\tau\mapsto F(\tau,\bm{H})
is
\big(\beta^{*}\gamma_{n}\big[\frac{R^{2}}{n\sigma}+\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\frac{R^{2}}{n}}}{2}\big]\big)\leq C\beta^{*}\gamma_{n}(\sigma+1)
-Lipschitz, we can conclude via an
\varepsilon
-net argument that there exists constants depending on
\beta^{*},\sigma
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\sup_{\tau\in[\sigma,\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+\frac{R^{2}}{n}}]}\bigg|F(\tau,\bm{H})-\mathbb{E}F(\tau,\bm{H})\bigg|\leq\varepsilon\frac{R\gamma_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{C\gamma_{n}^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}\exp\bigg(-cn\varepsilon^{2}\bigg)

(408)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\exp\bigg(-cn\varepsilon^{2}\bigg),

(409)

since
\gamma_{n}\geq 1
. Assuming this event holds we have that

\displaystyle\min_{\|\bm{W}\|_{F}\leq R}\widetilde{\ell}(\bm{W},\beta^{*})

\displaystyle\geq\min_{\tau\in[\sigma,\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+R^{2}}]}\mathbb{E}F(\tau,\bm{H})-\varepsilon\frac{R\gamma_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}.

(410)

Recalling that
R=C\sigma\sqrt{dr}
, it holds that
\varepsilon\frac{R\gamma_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}=C\varepsilon\sigma\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}
. Furthermore, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}F(\tau,\bm{H})

\displaystyle\geq\min_{\tau\in[\sigma,\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+R^{2}}]}\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2\tau}+\frac{\tau}{2}\bigg)\beta^{*}\gamma_{n}-\frac{\gamma_{n}(\beta^{*})^{2}}{2}

(411)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\min_{\bm{W}}\bigg\{\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{2\tau n}\|\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{n}\langle\bm{H,W}\rangle+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}\bigg\}\bigg\}

(412)

\displaystyle=\min_{\tau\in[\sigma,\sqrt{\sigma^{2}+R^{2}}]}\psi_{\lambda}^{*}(\tau,\beta^{*})

(413)

\displaystyle\geq\psi_{\lambda}^{*}(\tau^{*},\beta^{*})=L_{\lambda}^{*}

(414)

where
\psi_{\lambda}^{*}(\tau,\beta^{*})
is defined in (
315
). Combining these arguments, on these events we have that

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\geq L_{\lambda}^{*}-C\varepsilon\sqrt{\gamma_{n}}

(415)

which holds cumulatively with probability at least

\displaystyle 1-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\exp(-cn\varepsilon^{2})-\exp(-dr\varepsilon^{2}).

(416)

∎

C.2.2
Proof of
Lemma

16

For convenience we recall that
h(\bm{U})
is defined via

\displaystyle h(\bm{U}):=\frac{\gamma_{n}}{2}\bigg(\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n}\bigg)_{+}^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{U}\|_{F}^{2}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}.

(417)

We will define

\displaystyle\tau(\bm{U}):

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}},

(418)

which implies that

\displaystyle f(\bm{U})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n}\equiv\tau(\bm{U})\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n};

(419)

\displaystyle h(\bm{U})

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma_{n}}{2}(f(\bm{U}))_{+}^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{U}\|_{F}^{2}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}.

(420)

To prove
Lemma

16
we present the following lemma computing the second derivative of
h
.

Lemma 20
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg)\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(421)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}}\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(422)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}-2\bigg)\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(423)

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\big(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\big)}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(424)

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(425)

\displaystyle\quad+2\frac{\lambda}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(426)

Proof.

See
Section

C.2.6
.
∎

With this calculation in place we are free to prove
Lemma

16
.

Proof of
Lemma

16
.

Recall we define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{h}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{\big|\langle\bm{H},\bm{Q}\rangle\big|\leq C\sqrt{dr}\|\bm{Q}\|_{F}\text{ for all matrices $\bm{Q}$ of rank at most $2r$}\bigg\}\cap\big\{\frac{1}{2}\leq\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\leq 2\big\}\cap\bigg\{\|\bm{H}\|\leq 3\sqrt{d}\bigg\}.

(427)

We decompose the hessian by
Lemma

20
via

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]:=T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4}+T_{5}+T_{6}

(428)

where

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle:=\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg)\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(430)

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle:=\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}}\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(431)

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}-2\bigg)\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(432)

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle:=\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\big(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\big)}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(433)

\displaystyle T_{5}

\displaystyle:=\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(434)

\displaystyle T_{6}

\displaystyle:=2\frac{\lambda}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(435)

We will show that some of the terms above are small-order terms on the event
\mathcal{E}_{h}
, and then some of the terms provide a lower bound.

•

The term
T_{1}
: Observe that if
f(\bm{U})>0
,

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau(\bm{U})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n\tau(\bm{U})}.

(436)

In addition, we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}/\sqrt{n}\leq\tau(\bm{U}).

(437)

Consequently, we have that

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg|\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle\rangle}{n\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg|\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}}{n\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}

(438)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{dr}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg|\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle\rangle}{n\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg|\|\bm{U}\|^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(439)

Note that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{h}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg|\leq C\sqrt{dr}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F};\qquad\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\leq 2.

(440)

Therefore,

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8C}{\sqrt{ndr}}\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{n}\tau(\bm{U})}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}

(441)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8C}{\sqrt{ndr}}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(442)

Furthermore, when
\|\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{*}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}}\|\leq\frac{c}{\kappa(\bm{M})}\|\bm{U}^{*}\|
, we have that
\|\bm{U}\|\leq 2\|\bm{U}^{*}\|
. Therefore,

\displaystyle T_{1}\leq\frac{16C}{\sqrt{ndr}}\|\bm{M}\|\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}=\frac{16C\kappa}{\sqrt{ndr}}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(443)

•

The term
T_{2}
:
We note that the term
T_{2}
is a square of something times a nonnegative number, whence
T_{2}\geq 0
.

•

The term
T_{3}
: We will upper bound
T_{3}
. Recall that if
f(\bm{U})_{+}>0
then

\displaystyle f(\bm{U})_{+}=\tau(\bm{U})\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n}.

(444)

We have that

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}-2\bigg)\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(445)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{ndr\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{\tau(\bm{U})\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n}}{\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}-2\bigg)\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(446)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{ndr\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg(\frac{1}{\tau(\bm{U})}+\bigg|\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}\bigg|+2\bigg)\bigg|\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg|\bigg\langle(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M})\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(447)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{ndr\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg(\frac{1}{\tau(\bm{U})}+\bigg|\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}\bigg|+2\bigg)\bigg|\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg|\|(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M})\|_{F}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}\|.

(448)

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{h}
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{dr}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F};

(449)

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{dr}\|\bm{VU}^{\top}\|_{F}\lesssim\sqrt{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}\|\bm{U}\|.

(450)

Plugging this in above yields

\displaystyle T_{3}\lesssim\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{n\sqrt{dr}\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg(\frac{1}{\tau(\bm{U})}+\frac{\sqrt{dr}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{n\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}+2\bigg)\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}.

(451)

We also recall that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{n}\tau(\bm{U})}\leq 1.

(452)

Therefore,

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}}{\sqrt{ndr}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\tau(\bm{U})}+\frac{\sqrt{dr}}{\sqrt{n}\tau(\bm{U})}+2\bigg)

(453)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\bm{U}\|^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}}{\sqrt{ndr}}

(454)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{\kappa\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{\sqrt{ndr}}

(455)

since
\tau(\bm{U})\geq\sigma\geq\sigma_{\min}>0
and
\|\bm{U}\|^{2}\lesssim\|\bm{M}\|\lesssim\kappa\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})
.

•

The term
T_{4}
:
We will lower bound
T_{4}
. We have that

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{dr\tau(\bm{U})}f(\bm{U})_{+}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(456)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{dr\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg\langle\bigg(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle.

(457)

Next, we note that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{h}
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle\bigg|\leq C\sqrt{dr}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}.

(458)

In addition,
\tau(\bm{U})\geq\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{n}}
. Together, these conditions imply that

\displaystyle f(\bm{U})

\displaystyle=\tau(\bm{U})\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}--\frac{C\sqrt{dr}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{n}}

(459)

\displaystyle\geq.5\sigma+.5\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{C\sqrt{dr}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{n}}

(460)

\displaystyle=.5\sigma+\bigg(\frac{1}{2}-C\sqrt{\frac{dr}{n}}\bigg)\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{n}}

(461)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4}\sigma+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{n}},

(462)

where the final inequality holds by
2
as long as
C_{3}
is sufficiently large. Therefore, it holds that
\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau(\bm{U})}\geq\frac{1}{4}
, and hence that

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4dr}\bigg\langle\bigg(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle.

(463)

We now analyze the quantity
\bigg\langle\bigg(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle
. Observe that we can rewrite this quantity as

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{UV}^{\top}\bm{UV}^{\top}\big]\geq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}+{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{UV}^{\top}\bm{UV}^{\top}\big].

(464)

Weyl’s inequality implies that
\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}\geq\frac{1}{4}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})
provided
c
smaller than some absolute constant.

To complete the lower bound, we note that
\bm{V}=\bm{U-U^{\prime}}\mathcal{O}
, where
\mathcal{O}
is the Frobenius-optimal orthogonal matrix aligning
\bm{U}
and
\bm{U}^{\prime}
. Consequently, we observe that

\displaystyle{\sf Tr}\big[\bm{UV}^{\top}\bm{UV}^{\top}\big]

\displaystyle={\sf Tr}\big[\bm{U}(\bm{U-U^{\prime}}\mathcal{O})^{\top}\bm{U}(\bm{U-U^{\prime}}\mathcal{O})^{\top}\big]\geq 0,

(465)

where the inequality follows from Lemma 35 of
Ma et al. 2020
, which states that the matrix
\bm{U}(\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\prime}\mathcal{O})^{\top}
is a symmetric matrix. Combining all of these bounds we obtain

\displaystyle T_{4}\geq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{16dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(466)

•

The term
T_{5}
: We will upper bound
T_{5}
. First, we note that

\displaystyle T_{5}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU^{\top}-M-H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(467)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4f(\bm{U})_{+}}{dr\tau(\bm{U})}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}\|

(468)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{dr\tau(\bm{U})}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(\tau(\bm{U})\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{\langle\bm{H},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\rangle}{n}\bigg)\bigg(\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+\|\bm{H}\|\bigg)

(469)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}}{dr}\bigg(1+\frac{C\sqrt{dr}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}}{n\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg)\bigg(\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+\|\bm{H}\|\bigg)

(470)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}}{dr}\bigg(1+\frac{C\sqrt{dr}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg)\bigg(\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+\|\bm{H}\|\bigg)

(471)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}}{dr}\bigg(\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+\|\bm{H}\|\bigg).

(472)

Note that

\displaystyle\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|\leq 2\|\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{*}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}}\|\|\bm{U}^{*}\|+\|\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{*}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{*},\bm{U}}\|^{2}\leq\frac{3c}{\kappa(\bm{M})}\|\bm{U}^{*}\|^{2}

(473)

which implies that (since
\|\bm{H}\|\leq 3\sqrt{d}
on the event
\mathcal{E}_{h}
)

\displaystyle T_{5}\leq\frac{8\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}}{dr}\bigg(3c\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})+3\sqrt{d}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{24(c\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})+\sqrt{d})}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(474)

Under
1
it holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})\geq C_{1}\sigma\sqrt{dr}\geq C_{1}\sigma_{\min}\sqrt{dr}\geq c\sqrt{d}

(475)

as long as
C_{1}
is sufficiently large. Therefore,

\displaystyle T_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{48c\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(476)

•

The term
T_{6}
:
We will keep
T_{6}
as-is.

Combining all of our bounds, we obtain that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{1}{16dr}-\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{16C\kappa}{\sqrt{ndr}}

(477)

\displaystyle\quad-\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{C\kappa}{\sqrt{ndr}}-\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{48c}{dr}+\frac{2\lambda}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}

(478)

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{dr}\bigg(\frac{1}{16}-\frac{16C\kappa\sqrt{dr}}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{C\kappa\sqrt{dr}}{\sqrt{n}}-48c\bigg)+\frac{2\lambda}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}

(479)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}}{dr}\bigg(\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})\bigg[\frac{1}{16}-48c-\frac{17C\kappa\sqrt{dr}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg]+2\lambda\bigg)

(480)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}}{dr}\bigg(\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})\bigg[\frac{1}{16}-48c-\frac{17C}{\sqrt{C_{2}}}\bigg]+2\lambda\bigg)

(481)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}}{dr}\bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{32}+2\lambda\bigg),

(482)

where the penultimate inequality holds since
\kappa=O(1)
by
1
and
n\geq C_{3}dr^{2}
by
2
, and hence the final inequality holds as long as
C_{2}
larger than some absolute constant and
c
is smaller than some absolute constant.
∎

C.2.3
Proof of
Lemma

17

Proof of
Lemma

17
.

The proof follows via
\varepsilon
-net. Let
\bm{A}
be any rank at most
2r
matrix such that
\|\bm{A}\|_{F}\leq 1
. Then
\langle\bm{H},\bm{A}\rangle
is Gaussian with variance at most
2\|\bm{A}\|_{F}^{2}\leq 2
. Hoeffding’s inequality implies that
\bigg|\langle\bm{H},\bm{A}\rangle\bigg|\leq Ct
with probability at least
1-2\exp(-ct^{2})
. Next, let
\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
be a covering of rank at most
2r
matrices of Frobenius norm at most 1; by Lemma 3.1 of
Candès and Plan 2011
, it holds that
|\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}|\leq\bigg(\frac{9}{\varepsilon}\bigg)^{3dr}
. The union bound implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{A}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}}\bigg|\langle\bm{H},\bm{A}\rangle\bigg|\leq Ct

(483)

with probability at least
1-2\exp(3dr\log(9/\varepsilon)-t^{2})
. Taking
t\geq\sqrt{dr}
shows that this probability is at most
1-2\exp(-cdr)
as desired.

For the other event it holds that
\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}
is a
\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}
-Lipschitz function of
\bm{g}
and consequently the event holds with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cn)
. The bound on
\|\bm{H}\|
follows directly from
Bandeira and Handel 2016
.
∎

C.2.4
Proof of
Lemma

18

Proof of
Lemma

18
.

First, fix any
\bm{U}\in\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},K)
. Define the function

\displaystyle J:t\mapsto h(\bm{U}+t(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}-\bm{U})).

(484)

Then since

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt^{2}}J(t)

\displaystyle=(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}-\bm{U})^{\top}\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U}+t(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}-\bm{U})(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}-\bm{U})\geq\gamma\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}-\bm{U}\|_{F}^{2},

(485)

it holds that
J(t)
is strongly convex with parameter
\gamma\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}}-\bm{U}\|_{F}^{2}
.

Let
\bm{U}_{0}
be any minimizer of
h(\cdot)
within
\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},K)
. Then the condition of the lemma implies that
h(\bm{\widehat{U}})\leq h(\bm{U}_{0})+\varepsilon
. Applying the argument above with
\bm{U}=\bm{U}_{0}
implies that for any
s\in[0,1]
,

\displaystyle J(st_{1}+(1-s)t_{2})

\displaystyle\leq sJ(t_{1})+(1-s)J(t_{2})-\frac{1}{2}\gamma s(1-s)|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{2}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}-\bm{U}^{*}\|_{F}^{2}

(486)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow h(\bm{U}^{*}+s(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}-\bm{U}_{0}))

\displaystyle\leq sh(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O})+(1-s)h(\bm{U}_{0})-\frac{1}{2}\gamma s(1-s)\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}-\bm{U}_{0}\|_{F}^{2}

(487)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\frac{\gamma s(1-s)}{2}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}-\bm{U}_{0}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq sh(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}})+(1-s)h(\bm{U}_{0})-h(\bm{U}_{0}+s(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}-\bm{U}_{0}))

(488)

\displaystyle\leq s\big(h(\bm{U}_{0})+\varepsilon\big)+(1-s)h(\bm{U}_{0})-h(\bm{U}_{0}+s(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}-\bm{U}^{*}))

(489)

\displaystyle=s\varepsilon+h(\bm{U}_{0})-h(\bm{U}_{0}+s(\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}-\bm{U}_{0}))

(490)

\displaystyle\leq s\varepsilon.

(491)

Rearranging this bound implies that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}-\bm{U}_{0}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\varepsilon,

(492)

where we have implicitly used the fact that
h(\bm{U}\mathcal{O})=h(\bm{U})
for all orthogonal matrices
\mathcal{O}
. If
2\varepsilon/\gamma<K
, then
\bm{U}_{0}
is in the interior of
\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},K)
, and hence since
h
is strongly convex within
\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},K)
,
\bm{U}_{0}
is unique up to orthogonal transformation.

Now suppose
\bm{\widetilde{U}}\in\mathbb{B}(\bm{\widehat{U}},K)
is such that
h(\bm{\widetilde{U}})>h(\bm{U}_{0})+\varepsilon
.
Observe that if we redefine
J(t)
via
t\mapsto h(\bm{U}_{0}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}_{0},\bm{\widetilde{U}}}+t(\bm{\widetilde{U}}-\bm{U}_{0}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}_{0},\bm{\widetilde{U}}}))
, then via a similar argument,
J
is again strongly convex with parameter
\gamma\|\bm{\widetilde{U}}-\bm{U}_{0}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}_{0},\bm{\widetilde{U}}}\|_{F}^{2}
. However, we then have for all
s\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\gamma s(1-s)\|\bm{\widetilde{U}}-\bm{U}_{0}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}_{0},\bm{\widetilde{U}}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq sJ(1)+(1-s)J(0)-J(1)

(493)

\displaystyle=(s-1)h(\bm{\widetilde{U}})+(1-s)h(\bm{U}_{0})

(494)

\displaystyle\leq(s-1)h(\bm{U}_{0})+(s-1)\varepsilon+(1-s)h(\bm{U}_{0})

(495)

\displaystyle=(s-1)\varepsilon<0

(496)

which is a contradiction. Therefore, we must have that
h(\bm{\widetilde{U}})\leq h(\bm{U}_{0})+\varepsilon
, and hence by the same argument above must imply that
\|\bm{\widetilde{U}}-\bm{U}_{0}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}_{0},\bm{\widetilde{U}}}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\varepsilon
. Recalling that
\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}
is the Frobenius-norm minimizer shows that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\widetilde{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widetilde{U}},\bm{\widehat{U}}}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\widetilde{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}_{0},\bm{\widetilde{U}}}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}\|_{F}

(497)

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\widetilde{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}_{0},\bm{\widetilde{U}}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{0}\|_{F}+\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widehat{U}},\bm{U}_{0}}-\bm{U}_{0}\|_{F}

(498)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{2\varepsilon}{\gamma}}

(499)

which implies that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\widetilde{U}}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{\widetilde{U}},\bm{\widehat{U}}}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{8}{\gamma}\varepsilon

(500)

as desired.
∎

C.2.5
Proof of
Lemma

19

Proof.

We will argue similarly to the proof of
Lemma

15
. First, similar to the proof of that lemma, we have that

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}

\displaystyle(\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M})=\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2\tau^{*}}+\frac{\tau^{*}}{2}\bigg)\beta^{*}\gamma_{n}-\frac{\gamma_{n}(\beta^{*})^{2}}{2}

(501)

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\{\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{2\tau^{*}n}\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{\tau^{*}n}\langle\bm{H},\bm{Z}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\rangle+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{Z}^{\lambda/\zeta^{*}}(\tau^{*})\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}\bigg\}.

(502)

By
Lemma

11
, with probability at least
1-\exp(-cdr(\varepsilon\vee\varepsilon^{2}))
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\mathbb{E}\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}+\varepsilon dr.

(503)

Observe that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}

\displaystyle=(\tau^{*})^{2}-\sigma^{2}=O\bigg(\sigma^{2}\frac{dr}{n}\bigg),

(504)

where the final bound holds by
Lemma

6
.
Consequently, with probability at least
1-\exp(-cdr(\varepsilon\vee\varepsilon^{2}))
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}\leq Cdr+\varepsilon dr.

(505)

Take
\varepsilon\asymp 1
shows that with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cdr)
, one has
\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}=R^{\prime}
. Therefore, define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}:=\bigg\{\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\|_{F}\leq R^{\prime}\bigg\}.

(506)

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{1}
,

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M})

\displaystyle=\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2\tau^{*}}+\frac{\tau^{*}}{2}\bigg)\beta^{*}\gamma_{n}-\frac{\gamma_{n}(\beta^{*})^{2}}{2}

(507)

\displaystyle\qquad+\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|\bm{W}\|\leq R^{\prime}\\
\bm{W}\succcurlyeq-\bm{M}\end{subarray}}\bigg\{\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{2\tau^{*}n}\|\bm{W}\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{\tau^{*}n}\langle\bm{H},\bm{W}\rangle+\frac{\lambda}{dr}\big\{\|\bm{W}+\bm{M}\|_{*}-\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\big\}\bigg\}

(508)

\displaystyle=:\widetilde{L}_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{H}).

(509)

Notice that the function above is
\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{\tau^{*}n}R^{\prime}
- Lipschitz in
\bm{H}
. Define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}:=\bigg\{\bigg|\widetilde{L}_{\lambda}^{(n)}-\mathbb{E}_{\bm{H}}(\widetilde{L}_{\lambda}^{*})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon+C(\exp(-cdr))\bigg\},

(510)

where
C
and
c
are some universal constants.
By Lipschitz concentration it holds that

\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{2})\geq 1-2\exp\big(-\frac{(\varepsilon+C\exp(-cdr)\big)^{2}(\tau^{*})^{2}n^{2}}{\gamma_{n}^{2}(\beta^{*})^{2}(R^{\prime})^{2}})\geq 1-2\exp(-Cdr(C\exp(-cdr)+\varepsilon)^{2}).

We further observe that
L_{\lambda}^{*}=\mathbb{E}L_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|L_{\lambda}^{*}-\mathbb{E}\widetilde{L}_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{H})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{\gamma_{n}\beta^{*}}{\tau^{*}n}\|\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}+2\frac{\lambda}{dr}\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\bigg)\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathcal{E}_{1}^{c}\big\}

(511)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{\beta^{*}}{\tau^{*}dr}\|\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}+2\frac{C_{4}\sigma\sqrt{d}}{dr}\|\bm{M}\|_{*}\bigg)\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathcal{E}_{1}^{c}\big\}

(512)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{\beta^{*}}{\tau^{*}dr}r\kappa^{2}\lambda_{r}(\bm{M})^{2}+2\frac{C_{4}\sigma\sqrt{d}r\lambda_{r}(\bm{M})}{dr}\bigg)\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathcal{E}_{1}^{c}\big\}

(513)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{r\kappa^{2}d\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\tau^{*}dr}+2\frac{C_{4}\sigma dr\lambda_{\min}}{dr}\bigg)\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathcal{E}_{1}^{c}\big\}

(514)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(C_{2}\kappa^{2}\sigma^{2}r+2C_{4}\sqrt{C_{2}}\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r}\bigg)\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathcal{E}_{1}^{c}\big\}

(515)

\displaystyle=O\bigg(r\exp(-cdr)\bigg)

(516)

\displaystyle=O(\exp(-cdr)),

(517)

where we have applied
1
. Consequently it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|

\displaystyle L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\bigg)-L_{\lambda}^{*}\bigg|>\varepsilon+C\exp(-cdr)\bigg\}

(518)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|L_{\lambda}^{(n)}\bigg(\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda/\zeta^{*})}(\tau^{*})-\bm{M}\bigg)-L_{\lambda}^{*}\bigg|>\varepsilon+C\exp(-cdr)\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}\bigg\}+O\big(\exp(-cdr)\big)

(519)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\widetilde{L}_{\lambda}^{(n)}(\bm{H})-L_{\lambda}^{*}\bigg|>\varepsilon+C\exp(-cdr)\bigg\}+O\big(\exp(-cdr)\big)

(520)

\displaystyle\leq O\bigg(\exp\big(-Cdr\big(C\exp(-cdr)+\varepsilon\big)^{2}\big)+\exp(-cdr)\bigg).

(521)

This completes the proof.
∎

C.2.6
Proof of
Lemma

20

Proof of
Lemma

20
.

We observe that

\displaystyle\nabla h(\bm{U})

\displaystyle=\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}\nabla f(\bm{U})+\frac{2\lambda}{dr}\bm{U};

(522)

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\gamma_{n}\bigg\langle\nabla f(\bm{U}),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}+\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}\bigg\langle\frac{d}{dt}\nabla f(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg|_{t=0}+2\frac{\lambda}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(523)

Therefore, in order to calculate
\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]
, we need the derivative of
f(\bm{U})
. We have that

\displaystyle\nabla\tau(\bm{U})

\displaystyle=2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M})}{n}\bm{U};

(524)

\displaystyle\nabla f(\bm{U})

\displaystyle=\nabla\tau(\bm{U})\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{2\bm{HU}}{n};\equiv 2\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M})}{n}\bm{U}-\frac{2\bm{HU}}{n},

(525)

which implies that

\displaystyle\langle\nabla f(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}),\bm{V}\rangle

(526)

\displaystyle=2\tau(\bm{U}+t\bm{V})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\frac{(\bm{U}+t\bm{V})(\bm{U}+t\bm{V})^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle.

(527)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}

\displaystyle\langle\nabla f(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}),\bm{V}\rangle\bigg|_{t=0}

(528)

\displaystyle=2\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\frac{d}{dt}\tau(\bm{U}+t\bm{V})^{-1}\bigg|_{t=0}

(529)

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{d}{dt}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}+t\bm{VU}^{\top}+t\bm{UV}^{\top}+t^{2}\bm{XX}^{\top}\big)-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg|_{t=0}

(530)

\displaystyle=2\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\frac{d}{dt}\tau(\bm{U}+t\bm{V})^{-1}\bigg|_{t=0}

(531)

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\big(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\big)}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(532)

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(533)

We can compute

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\tau(\bm{U}+t\bm{V})^{-1}\bigg|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=-1\bigg[\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}{n}+\sigma^{2}\bigg]^{-3/2}\frac{\langle\bm{UU^{\top}-M},\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\rangle}{n}

(534)

\displaystyle=-\tau(\bm{U})^{-3}\frac{\langle\bm{UU^{\top}-M},\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\rangle}{n}.

(535)

This gives us the expression:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\langle\nabla f(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}),\bm{V}\rangle\bigg|_{t=0}

\displaystyle=-2\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\tau(\bm{U})^{-3}\frac{\langle\bm{UU^{\top}-M},\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\rangle}{n}

(536)

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\big(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\big)}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(537)

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(538)

Therefore:

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\gamma_{n}\bigg\langle\nabla f(\bm{U}),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}+\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}\bigg\langle\frac{d}{dt}\nabla f(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg|_{t=0}+2\frac{\lambda}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}

(539)

\displaystyle=\gamma_{n}\bigg\langle 2\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}}{n}\bm{U}-\frac{2\bm{HU}}{n},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(540)

\displaystyle\quad-2\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\tau(\bm{U})^{-3}\frac{\langle\bm{UU^{\top}-M},\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\rangle}{n}

(541)

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\big(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\big)}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(542)

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(543)

\displaystyle\quad+2\frac{\lambda}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(544)

We now expand out the first two terms. We have that

\displaystyle\gamma_{n}\bigg\langle 2\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}}{n}\bm{U}-\frac{2\bm{HU}}{n},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|^{2}}{n}\bigg\langle(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M})\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(545)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}}\bigg\langle\bm{H}\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(546)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{8\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle.

(547)

Similarly,

\displaystyle-2\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\tau(\bm{U})^{-3}\frac{\langle\bm{UU^{\top}-M},\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\rangle}{n}

(548)

\displaystyle=-2\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\tau(\bm{U})^{-3}\frac{\langle\bm{UU^{\top}-M},\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\rangle}{n}

(549)

\displaystyle\quad+2\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\tau(\bm{U})^{-3}\frac{\langle\bm{UU^{\top}-M},\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\rangle}{n}

(550)

\displaystyle=\frac{-4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(551)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle.

(552)

Adding these two terms together we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}5\bigg(\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg)\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(553)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}}\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(554)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}-2\bigg)\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(555)

Therefore, we have the final expression:

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}h(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau(\bm{U})}\bigg)\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(556)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}}\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle^{2}

(557)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma_{n}}{n^{2}\tau(\bm{U})}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{f(\bm{U})_{+}}{\tau^{2}(\bm{U})}-2\bigg)\bigg\langle\bm{HU},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(558)

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\big(\bm{VU}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\big)}{n}\bigg)\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(559)

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma_{n}f(\bm{U})_{+}2\tau(\bm{U})^{-1}\frac{\|\bm{g}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bigg(\frac{\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}-\bm{H}}{n}\bigg)\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(560)

\displaystyle\quad+2\frac{\lambda}{dr}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(561)

∎

Appendix D
Proofs of Lemmas in
Section

5

In this section we prove the two intermediate lemmas required for the proof of
Theorem

7
.

Proof of
Lemma

9

To prove
Lemma

9
we first require the following lemma.

Lemma 21
.

Let
\{\bm{X}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}\sim{\sf GOE}(d)
, and let
\mathcal{X}(\cdot)
be the operator that assigns entries
\mathcal{X}(\cdot)_{i}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\langle\bm{X}_{i},\cdot\rangle
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{V}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}:\|\bm{V}\|\leq 1}\bigg\|\big(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\big)\big(\bm{VV}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|\lesssim\frac{r\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{n}}

(562)

with probability at least
1-2\exp(-C_{1}dr)-2\exp(-C_{2}n)
.

Proof.

See
Section

D.1.1
.
∎

We now have the proof of
Lemma

9
.

Proof of
Lemma

9
.

Note that
\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}
satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i}\bigg(\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle/\sqrt{n}-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg)\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}+\lambda\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}=0,

(563)

which shows that
\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}_{({\sf deb})}
satisfies

\displaystyle\nabla f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}

\displaystyle(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}_{{\sf deb}})

(564)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i}\bigg\{\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle/\sqrt{n}-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)^{1/2}

(565)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i}\bigg\{\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle/\sqrt{n}-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)^{1/2}

(566)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lambda}{n}\sum_{i}\bigg\{\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bigg\}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)^{1/2}

(567)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i}\bigg\{\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle/\sqrt{n}-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)^{1/2}

(568)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}\bigg(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big((\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bigg)\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)^{1/2}

(569)

\displaystyle=-\lambda\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)^{1/2}

(570)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\bigg[\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big((\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bigg]\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)^{1/2}

(571)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)^{1/2}

(572)

\displaystyle=\lambda\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\bigg[\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big((\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bigg]\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\bigg(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\bigg)^{1/2}.

(573)

Taking norms, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|\nabla f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}_{{\sf deb}}\big)\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\big\|\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big(\bm{I}+\lambda\big(\bm{U}^{(\lambda)\top}\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}\big)^{-1}\big)^{1/2}\big\|_{F}\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\big(\bm{V}_{U}\bm{V}_{U}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|

(574)

\displaystyle=\lambda\|\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}_{{\sf deb}}\|_{F}\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\big(\bm{V}_{U}\bm{V}_{U}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|.

(575)

By
Lemma

21
, we have that with the advertised probability,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\big(\bm{V}_{U}\bm{V}_{U}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{n}},

(576)

whence the proof is completed.
∎

D.1.1
Proof of
Lemma

21

Proof of
Lemma

21
.

We will proceed via
\varepsilon
-net argument. First, let
\bm{V}
be any fixed
d\times r
orthonormal matrix. We have that

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}\big(\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}

(577)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bigg(\bm{I}-\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}

(578)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}

(579)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\bigg(\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bigg)\bigg(\bm{I}-\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}

(580)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\bigg(\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bigg)\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}

(581)

As a result,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\big(\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\bigg(\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bigg)\bigg(\bm{I}-\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bigg)\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bigg\|

(582)

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|\bm{V}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\bigg(\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bigg)\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\bigg\|

(583)

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=T_{1}+T_{2},

(584)

where we have used the rotational invariance of
\|\cdot\|
. We analyze each term in turn.

•

The quantity
T_{1}
:
By rotational invariance the quantities
\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}
and
(\bm{I-VV}^{\top})\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}
are independent from each other, and hence each quantity inside the summation is a product of two independent random variables. Let
\bm{V}_{\perp}
denote the
d\times(d-r)
matrix such that
\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{V}=0
, and let
c_{i}={\sf Tr}\big(\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\big)
. We may then write the summation as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}\bm{V}_{\perp}\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}

\displaystyle\overset{d}{=}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}\bm{V}_{\perp}\bm{H}_{i},

(585)

where
\bm{H}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d-r\times r}
is a matrix with
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
entries. Furthermore, we have that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}\bm{V}_{\perp}\bm{H}_{i}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}\bm{H}_{i}\bigg\|.

(586)

So it suffices to bound the term above. To do so, we proceed via
\varepsilon
-net. let
\bm{x},\bm{y}
be
d-
dimensional and
r
-dimensional unit vectors respectively. Then it holds that conditional on
\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{H}_{i}\bm{y}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{H}_{i}\bm{y}\sim\mathcal{N}\bigg(0,\frac{r}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{H}_{i}\bm{y}\big)^{2}\bigg).

(587)

As a result, Hoeffding’s inequality implies that with probability at least
1-2\exp(-ct^{2})
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{H}_{i}\bm{y}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim t\frac{\sqrt{r}}{n}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{H}_{i}\bm{y}\big)^{2}}.

(588)

In addition, note that
\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{H}_{i}\bm{y}\big)^{2}}
is simply the norm of a standard Gaussian random variable in
n
-dimensions. Let the event
\mathcal{E}
be denoted

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}:=\bigg\{\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{H}_{i}\bm{y}\big)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{n}\bigg\}.

(589)

Then
\mathcal{E}
holds with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cn)
. Conditional on
\mathcal{E}
, with probability at least
1-2\exp(-ct^{2})
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{H}_{i}\bm{y}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim t\frac{\sqrt{r}}{\sqrt{n}}

(590)

By taking the supremum over a
1/4
-net, we have that by redefining
t=C(\sqrt{d}+t)
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}\bm{H}_{i}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\bigg(\sqrt{d}+t\bigg)

(591)

with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cdt^{2})-2\exp(C_{1}d-cn)
. Under the assumption
n\gg dr
, this probability is at least
1-2\exp(-cdt^{2})-2\exp(-cn)
.

•

The quantity
T_{2}
:
We note that
\mathbb{E}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\big(\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\big)\bm{VV}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}=\bm{VV}^{\top}
. Let
\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}
denote the random variable
\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}
. Then
\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}
is equal in distributionn to an
r\times r
GOE random matrix. We have that

\displaystyle\bm{V}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\big(\bm{V}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}\big)\bm{VV}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V}

\displaystyle\overset{d}{=}\bm{V}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)\bm{V}\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}

(592)

\displaystyle=\bm{V}\bigg(\bm{I}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\bigg).

(593)

Consequently,

\displaystyle\|\bm{V}\bigg(\bm{I}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\bigg)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{I}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\|,

(594)

since
\|\bm{V}\|=1
. Let
\bm{x}
by any fixed
r
-dimensional unit vector. Then

\displaystyle\bm{x}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{I}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\sf Tr}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\bigg)\bm{x}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{r}x_{l}^{2}\bigg((\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i})_{ll}^{2}-1\bigg)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{r}\sum_{j\neq l}x_{j}^{2}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)_{ll}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)_{jj}

(595)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{r}\sum_{j<k,j\neq l}2\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)_{ll}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)_{jk}\bm{x}_{j}\bm{x}_{k}

(596)

\displaystyle=:J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}.

(597)

We bound each of these quantities separately.

–

The term
J_{1}
:
We note that
J_{1}
is simply a sum of
nr
independent rescaled
\chi^{2}
random variables. By Lemma 1 of
Laurent and Massart 2000
it holds that

\displaystyle J_{1}\leq 2\frac{\sqrt{\sum_{l=1}^{r}x_{l}^{4}}}{n}t+4\frac{r}{n}t^{2}\leq 2\frac{t}{n}+4\frac{r}{n}t^{2}

(598)

with probability at least
1-\exp(-t^{2})
.

–

The term
J_{2}
:
Note that we can write

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1,l=1\\
j\neq l\end{subarray}}^{r}\bm{A}_{lj}\langle\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{l},\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{j}\rangle,

where
\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{l}
is the
n
-dimensional vector with coordinates given by
(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i})_{ll}
, and
\bm{A}_{lj}=\frac{x_{j}^{2}}{n}
.
Note that
\bm{A}
is the matrix whose rows are all given by
[\frac{x_{1}^{2}}{n},\frac{x_{2}^{2}}{n}\cdots\frac{x_{r}^{2}}{n}]
. It is then evident that
\|\bm{A}\|=\frac{1}{n}
and

\displaystyle\|\bm{A}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=r\sum_{j}\frac{x_{j}^{4}}{n^{2}}\leq\frac{r}{n^{2}}.

By Proposition 2.5 of
Chen and Yang 2021
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big\{|J_{2}|>Ct\big\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg(-Cn\min\bigg[\frac{t^{2}}{r},t\bigg]\bigg).

Redefining
t=t\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle|J_{2}|\leq Ct\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}

(599)

with probability at least
1-2\exp(-ct^{2})
for all
t\leq\sqrt{nr}
.

–

The term
J_{3}
:
We note that

\displaystyle J_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{r}2\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)_{ll}\sum_{j<k,j\neq l}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)_{jk}\bm{x}_{j}\bm{x}_{k}

(600)
