\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j<k,j\neq l}2\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)_{jk}x_{j}x_{k}y_{i},

(601)

where
y_{i}=\sum_{l=1}^{r}\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)_{ll}.
Conditional on
y
, Hoeffding’s inequality implies that with probability at least
1-2\exp(-ct^{2})
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j<k,j\neq l}2\big(\bm{\widetilde{H}}_{i}\big)_{jk}x_{j}x_{k}y_{i}\bigg|\leq t\frac{C}{n}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}y_{i}^{2}}.

Observe that each
y_{i}
is a Gaussian random variable with variance
r
. Consequently, with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cn)-2\exp(-Ct^{2})
it holds that

\displaystyle t\frac{C}{n}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}y_{i}^{2}}\leq C\frac{t\sqrt{r}}{\sqrt{n}}.

Combining all these bounds, we obtain that with probability at least
1-O\bigg(\exp(-ct^{2})-2\exp(-cn)\bigg)
for all
t\leq\sqrt{nr}
,

\displaystyle|J_{1}|+|J_{2}|+|J_{3}|\lesssim t\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}+\frac{t}{n}+\frac{r}{n}t^{2}

Define
t=C(\sqrt{r}+s)
. Then a net argument shows that for all
s\leq c\sqrt{nr}
,

\displaystyle|J_{1}|+|J_{2}|+|J_{3}|\lesssim(\sqrt{r}+s)\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{r}+s}{n}+\frac{r}{n}(r+s^{2}),

(602)

which holds with probability at least

\displaystyle 1-\exp(-cs^{2})-2\exp(-cn),

where we have adjusted constants as necessary, using the fact that
r\leq n
by assumption.

Combining the bounds for
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
, we see that with probability at least
1-O\bigg(\exp(-cs^{2})-\exp(-cn)-\exp(-dt^{2})-\exp(-cn)\bigg)
it holds that for any fixed
\bm{V}
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)(\bm{VV}^{\top})\bigg\|\lesssim(\sqrt{r}+s)\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{r}+s}{n}+\frac{r}{n}(r+s^{2})+\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\bigg(\sqrt{d}+t\bigg),

provided
s\leq c\sqrt{nr}
. Let
\{\mathcal{V}_{\varepsilon}\}
denote an
\varepsilon
-net of the set of matrices
\bm{V}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
with respect to the metric
d(\bm{V}_{1},\bm{V}_{2})=\|\bm{V}_{1}\bm{V}_{1}^{\top}-\bm{V}_{2}\bm{V}_{2}^{\top}\|
, which has cardinality at most
\big(\frac{4+\varepsilon}{\varepsilon}\big)^{dr}
. Let

\displaystyle\bm{\bar{V}}:=\argmax_{\bm{V}}\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\big(\bm{VV}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|.

(603)

and set

\displaystyle M:=\max\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\big(\bm{VV}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|.

(604)

Let
\bm{\bar{V}}_{\varepsilon}
denote the quantity in the
\varepsilon
-net that is
\varepsilon
-close to
\bm{\bar{V}}
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\big(\bm{\bar{V}\bar{V}}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|

(605)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\big(\bm{\bar{V}\bar{V}}^{\top}-\bm{\bar{V}}_{\varepsilon}\bm{\bar{V}}_{\varepsilon}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|

(606)

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{X}^{*}\mathcal{X}-\mathcal{I}\bigg)\big(\bm{\bar{V}}_{\varepsilon}\bm{\bar{V}}_{\varepsilon}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|

(607)

\displaystyle\leq M\varepsilon+C(\sqrt{r}+s)\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{r}+s}{n}+\frac{r}{n}(r+s^{2})+\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\bigg(\sqrt{d}+t\bigg)

(608)

which implies that

\displaystyle M\lesssim(\sqrt{r}+s)\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{r}+s}{n}+\frac{r}{n}(r+s^{2})+\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\bigg(\sqrt{d}+t\bigg)

(609)

with probability at least

1-O\bigg(\exp(dr\log((4+\varepsilon)/\varepsilon)-cs^{2})-\exp(dr\log((4+\varepsilon)/\varepsilon)-cn)-\exp(dr\log((4+\varepsilon)/\varepsilon)-dt^{2})\bigg).

Taking
\varepsilon=\frac{1}{4}
and
t,s\asymp\sqrt{dr}
(and further noting that
s\leq c\sqrt{nr}
) shows that with the advertised probability,

\displaystyle M\lesssim\frac{r\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{dr}}{n}+\frac{dr^{2}}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\sqrt{dr}\lesssim\frac{r\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{n}},

where the final bound holds by
2
, since
n\geq C_{3}dr^{2}
.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

10

The proof of this result relies on the following lemmas. The first lemma provides the form of the second derivative.

Lemma 22
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{UV}^{\top}+\bm{VU}^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(610)

\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(611)

Proof.

See
Section

D.2.1
.
∎

Define the objective function

\displaystyle f^{{\sf MF}}(\bm{U})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}.

(612)

A straightforward calculation shows that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{{\sf MF}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=.5\|\bm{UV}^{\top}+\bm{UV}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}+\bigg\langle\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M},\bm{VV}^{\top}\bigg\rangle.

(613)

To prove
Lemma

10
we first decompose
\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]
via

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\nabla^{2}f^{{\sf MF}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]+\bigg(\nabla^{2}f^{{\sf MF}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]-\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\bigg).

(614)

The following lemma shows that the second term is sufficiently small with high probabiltiy.

Lemma 23
.

The following bound holds uniformly for all
\bm{V}
and
\bm{U}
of rank at most
2r
with probability at least
1-O\big(\exp(-cdr)+\exp(-cd)\big)
:

\displaystyle\bigg|\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]-\nabla^{2}f^{{\sf MF}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\bigg|\leq\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(2\delta_{2r}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{M}\|_{F}+C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\bigg),

(615)

where
\delta_{2r}
is as in
Lemma

1
.

Proof.

See
Section

D.2.2
.
∎

Finally, the next lemma provides a deterministic bound on
\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]
for appropriate choice of
\bm{U}
on the event from
Lemma

23
.

Lemma 24
.

Suppose
\mathcal{E}_{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:secondderivcloseness}}
holds.
Let
\bm{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
, and let
\bm{V}=\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\prime}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{\prime},\bm{U}}
. Then
it holds that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\geq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(1-\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+2\delta_{2r}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{M}\|+C\sigma\sqrt{dr}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}}\bigg)

(616)

and

\displaystyle\bigg|\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\bigg|\leq\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+2\delta_{2r}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{f}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{M}\|_{F}+C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\bigg).

(617)

Proof.

See
Section

D.2.3
.
∎

With these lemmas in hand we are prepared to prove
Lemma

10
.

Proof of
Lemma

10
.

We observe that

\displaystyle{\sf vec}(\bm{U}^{(0)}-\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)})^{\top}\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}\big(t\bm{U}^{(0)}+(1-t)\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}_{{\sf deb}}\big){\sf vec}(\bm{U}^{(0)}-\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)})

\displaystyle=\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}\bigg(t\bm{U}^{(0)}+(1-t)\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}\bigg)[\bm{V},\bm{V}],

(618)

with
\bm{V}=\bm{U}^{(0)}-\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)},
where we have already assumed (without loss of generality) that
\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{(0)},\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}}=\bm{I}_{r}
. Let
\bm{U}=t\bm{U}^{(0)}+(1-t)\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}_{{\sf deb}}
. By
Lemma

24
, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:secondderivcloseness}}
it holds that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}\big(\bm{U}\big)[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\geq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(1-\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+2\delta_{2r}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{M}\|+C\sigma\sqrt{dr}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}}\bigg).

(619)

To complete the proof of the lower bound we need only bound the numerator above.

•

Step 1: Studying
\bm{U}^{(0)}
and
\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}
: Through an argument similar to the proof of
Lemma

3
(take
\lambda=0
therein) shows that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf Good}}

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}^{(0)}\bm{U}^{(0)\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}.

(620)

Therefore, Weyl’s inequality shows that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{(0)})^{2}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{(0)}\bm{U}^{(0)\top})\geq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})-C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\geq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{2},

(621)

which holds by
1
.

We now consider
\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}
. We have that by
Theorem

5
it holds that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf Good}}

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)\top}=\bm{Z}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}=\bm{V}_{U}\big(\bm{\Sigma}+\lambda\bm{I}\big)\bm{V}_{U}^{\top}.

(622)

Furthermore, on this same event, by
Theorem

2
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{Z}^{(\lambda)}-\bm{M}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}.

(623)

Consequently, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}\leq C\sigma\sqrt{dr}.

(624)

Therefore, the same argument shows that
\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)})^{2}\geq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{2}
. The triangle inequality also shows that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}\|^{2}\leq\|\bm{M}\|+\|\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}\leq 2\|\bm{M}\|.

(625)

The same bound holds for
\bm{U}^{(0)}
as well.

•

Step 2: Bounding
\bm{U}
.
As a consequence of the previous step, we have that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\min}\bigg(t\bm{U}^{(0)}+(1-t)\bm{U}^{(\lambda)}_{{\sf deb}}\bigg)^{2}

(626)

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\min}\bigg(t^{2}\bm{U}^{(0)\top}\bm{U}^{(0)}+(1-t)^{2}\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)\top}\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}+t(1-t)\bm{U}^{(0)\top}\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}+t(1-t)\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)\top}\bm{U}^{(0)}\bigg)

(627)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{t^{2}}{2}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})+\frac{(1-t)^{2}}{2}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})+2t(1-t)\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)\top}\bm{U}^{(0)})

(628)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{t^{2}+(1-t)^{2}}{2}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})

(629)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{8},

(630)

where we have recognized that
\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)\top}\bm{U}^{(0)}
is a positive diagonal matrix by the fact that they are already aligned. A similar argument gives

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}\|^{2}\leq 4\|\bm{M}\|.

(631)

By Lemma 6 of
Ge et al. 2017
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}^{(0)}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{(0)},\bm{U}^{*}}-\bm{U}^{*}\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{2\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\bm{M})}\|\bm{U}^{(0)}\bm{U}^{(0)\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}\leq\frac{C\sigma\sqrt{dr}}{2\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\bm{M})}.

(632)

•

Step 3: Bounding
\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}
.

First, define the set

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}_{x}(\bm{U}^{*}):=\{\bm{U}:\|\bm{U}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U},\bm{U}^{*}}-\bm{U}^{*}\|_{F}\leq x\}.

(633)

We note that
\mathbb{B}_{x}(\bm{U}^{*})
is the geodesic ball on the Riemannian quotient space
[\bm{U}]:=\{\bm{U}\mathcal{O}:\mathcal{OO}^{\top}=\bm{I}_{r}\}
. By Theorem 2 of
Luo and Trillos 2022
, the set
\mathbb{B}_{x}(\bm{U}^{*})
is geodesically convex for any
x\leq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{*})/3
. Moreover, by Lemma 2 of
Luo and Trillos 2022
the function

\displaystyle\bm{U}(t):=\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}+t\big(\bm{U}^{(0)}-\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}\big)

(634)

is the unique minimizing geodesic from
[\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}]
to
[\bm{U}^{(0)}]
(where we have used the fact that without loss of generality both matrices are already properly aligned). Since both
\bm{U}^{(0)}
and
\bm{U}_{{\sf deb}}^{(\lambda)}
are full rank from the previous arguments, it holds that
[\bm{U}]\in\mathbb{B}_{x}(\bm{U}^{*})
. In particular, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}(t)\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U},\bm{U}^{*}}-\bm{U}^{*}\|_{F}\leq\frac{C\sigma\sqrt{dr}}{2\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\bm{M})}.

(635)

for all
t
. Lemma 11 of
Luo and Trillos 2022
shows that

\displaystyle\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|\leq\sqrt{\kappa}C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\leq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{10},

(636)

where the final bound follows from
1
.

Combining these bounds we have that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\sf Good}}

\displaystyle\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+2\delta_{2r}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{M}\|+C\sigma\sqrt{dr}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\kappa}C\sigma\sqrt{dr}+8\frac{c_{0}}{\sqrt{r}}\|\bm{M}\|+4c_{0}\|\bm{M}\|+C\sigma\sqrt{dr}

(637)

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\sigma\sqrt{dr}+C^{\prime}c_{0}\|\bm{M}\|

(638)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{20}

(639)

provided
c_{0}
is smaller than some constant, where
c_{0}
is such that
\delta_{2r}\leq\frac{c_{0}}{\sqrt{r}}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\geq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{8}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\big(1-\frac{2}{5}\big)\geq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{M})}{20}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}

(640)

as desired. The upper bound follows from a similar argument.

∎

D.2.1
Proof of
Lemma

22

Proof of
Lemma

22
.

The hessian satisfies

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg]\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle.

(641)

We have that

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle

\displaystyle\bm{X}_{i},\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg]\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(642)

\displaystyle=\bigg[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg]\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(643)

\displaystyle=\bigg[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg]\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(644)

\displaystyle\quad+t\bigg[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg]\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(645)

\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(646)

\displaystyle=\bigg[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{U}\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg]\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(647)

\displaystyle\quad+t\bigg[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{V}\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bigg]\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(648)

\displaystyle\quad+t\bigg[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big)^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg]\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(649)

\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\big(\bm{U}+t\bm{V}\big),\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(650)

Taking only terms linear in
t
yields that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{UW}^{\top}+\bm{VU}^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(651)

\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle

(652)

as desired.
∎

D.2.2
Proof of
Lemma

23

Proof of
Lemma

23
.

First, we write

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]:=\nabla^{2}f^{({\sf clean})}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle,

(653)

where

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{({\sf clean})}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{UV}^{\top}+\bm{VU}^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{U},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle+\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\bigg\rangle\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i}\bm{V},\bm{V}\bigg\rangle.

(654)

First we bound the difference between
\nabla^{2}f^{{\sf clean}}
and
\nabla^{2}f^{{\sf MF}}
, uniformly over matrices of rank at most
2r
. Lemma 3 of
Chi et al. 2019
shows that if
\mathcal{X}
satisfies the restricted isometry property with constant
\delta_{2r}
, then

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{Q}_{1}\rangle\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{Q}_{2}\rangle-\langle\bm{Q}_{1},\bm{Q}_{2}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{2r}\|\bm{Q}_{1}\|_{F}\|\bm{Q}_{2}\|_{F},

(655)

uniformly over matrices
\bm{Q}_{1},\bm{Q}_{2}
of rank at most
r
. Consequently, when the restricted isometry property holds, by breaking up the sum it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\nabla^{2}f^{{(\sf clean)}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]-\nabla^{2}f^{{\sf MF}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{2r}\bigg(2\|\bm{UV}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}\|\bm{VV}^{\top}\|_{F}+\|\bm{M}\|_{F}\|\bm{VV}^{\top}\|_{F}\bigg)

(656)

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{2r}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(2\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}+\|\bm{M}\|_{F}\bigg).

(657)

We now bound the noise term. Observe that for fixed matrix
\bm{VV}^{\top}
of rank at most
r
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{d}{=}\frac{\|\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{n}}\langle\bm{\varepsilon},\bm{Z}\rangle,

(658)

where
\bm{Z}
and
\bm{\varepsilon}/\sigma
are standard Gaussian random vectors. Standard concentration arguments show that for fixed matrix
\bm{VV}^{\top}
, conditional on
\bm{\varepsilon}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma\|\bm{VV}^{\top}\|_{F}\frac{\|\bm{\varepsilon}\|}{\sqrt{n}}s

(659)

with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cs^{2})
. Recognizing that
\|\bm{\varepsilon}\|/\sqrt{n}\leq C
with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cn)
shows that for any fixed
\bm{V}
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i}\bigg\langle\bm{X}_{i},\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\top}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma\|\bm{VV}^{\top}\|_{F}s

(660)

with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cs^{2})-2\exp(-cn)
. Taking
s\asymp\sqrt{dr}
and a standard net argument shows that this bound holds uniformly over matrices
\bm{VV}^{\top}
of rank at most
r
with probability at least
1-O(\exp(-cdr))
.
∎

D.2.3
Proof of
Lemma

24

Proof of
Lemma

24
.

We have that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{.5\|\bm{UV}^{\top}+\bm{VU}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}}_{=:T_{1}}+\underbrace{\langle\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M},\bm{VV}^{\top}\rangle}_{=:T_{2}}+\underbrace{\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]-\nabla^{2}f^{{(\sf MF)}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]}_{=:T_{3}}.

(661)

We will upper and lower bound
T_{1}
and upper bound
T_{2}
and
T_{3}
.

•

The Term
T_{1}
: The upper bound

\displaystyle|T_{1}|\leq\|\bm{U}\|^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}

(662)

is immediate. For the lower bound, we have

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=.5\|\bm{U}\bm{V}^{\top}+\bm{V}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

(663)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{U}\bm{V}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{V}\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}+{\sf Tr}\bigg(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{V}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{V}\bigg)

(664)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}+{\sf Tr}\bigg(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{V}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{V}\bigg)

(665)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2},

(666)

where the final inequality follows by Lemma 35 of
Ma et al. 2020
as
\bm{V}=\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\prime}\mathcal{O}_{\bm{U}^{\prime},\bm{U}}
.

•

The term
T_{2}
:
We have that

\displaystyle|T_{2}|

\displaystyle=\langle\big(\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\big)\bm{V},\bm{V}\rangle\leq\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}.

(667)

•

The term
T_{3}
:
Let
\mathcal{E}_{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:secondderivcloseness}}
denote the event from
Lemma

23
. On this event we have that

\displaystyle|T_{3}|\leq\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(2\delta_{2r}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{M}\|_{F}+C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\bigg).

(668)

Combining bounds we have that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\geq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(1-\frac{\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+2\delta_{2r}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{F}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{M}\|+C\sigma\sqrt{dr}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U})^{2}}\bigg).

(669)

For the upper bound we have that

\displaystyle\bigg|\nabla^{2}f^{(0)}_{{\sf ncvx}}(\bm{U})[\bm{V},\bm{V}]\bigg|\leq\|\bm{V}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\|\bm{UU}^{\top}-\bm{M}\|+2\delta_{2r}\|\bm{U}\|^{2}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{UU}^{\top}\|_{f}+\delta_{2r}\|\bm{M}\|_{F}+C\sigma\sqrt{dr}\bigg).

(670)

∎

Appendix E
Proof of Corollary
1

Proof.

By
Theorem

1
, when
\frac{n}{dr^{2}}\to\infty
with
\gamma_{n}/(dr)\to 0
it holds that with probability tending to one

\displaystyle\frac{1}{dr}\bigg|\|\bm{Z}^{(\lambda)}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda)}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle=o(1),

with the same result holding for
\bm{U}^{(0)}\bm{U}^{(0)\top}
and
\bm{Z}_{{\sf HT}}
respectively (to make this function Lipschitz, strictly speaking we first restrict to a bounded set and consider its Lipschitz extension, but this restriction is minor). Consequently, since the right hand side above is
o(1)
, we have in probability

\displaystyle\liminf\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{(\lambda)}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}{\|\bm{U}^{(0)}\bm{U}^{(0)\top}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\mathbb{E}_{\bm{H}}\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda)}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}_{\bm{H}}\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf HT}}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}}.

Therefore, we need only consider the mean squared error for
\bm{Z}_{{\sf HT}}
and
\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda)}
. When
r
is fixed, this is given in Lemma 4 of
Gavish and Donoho 2014
. Matching notation and rescaling appropriately to their asymptotic framework, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d\sigma^{2}}\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf HT}}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\to 2r+\sum_{i=1}^{r}3\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{i}^{2}};

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d\sigma^{2}}\|\bm{Z}_{{\sf ST}}^{(\lambda)}-\bm{M}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\to 2r+\sum_{i=1}^{r}3\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\big(\lambda_{i}-\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{d}}\big)^{2}},

where the convergence is almost surely. Since the right hand side is a constant, we conclude by the Dominated Convergence Theorem that the same limits hold for the expectations. Since
\lambda_{i}>\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{d}}
by
1
and
3
, it holds that
\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{i}^{2}}\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{(\lambda_{i}-\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{d}}})^{2}
, whence the result is immediate. Strict inequality for any choices of
\lambda_{i}
satisfying
1
follows.
∎
